Thus, the appropriate statement is: gravity deprojects not when gravity vanishes, but when the relation hidden by the gravitational projection becomes jointly readable again.

A weaker dimensional gravitational calibration can help move the physical read in that direction because

$$R_{atm} \propto G_{n}.$$

It is not itself the definition of deprojection.

### 11.14 Force and Geometry Become Optional Coordinates
The conventional molecular description of an atmosphere says that each molecule experiences gravitational force

$$F_{g} = mg.$$

That description remains valid. But the same observable atmospheric organization can be stated without assigning primitive causal agency to that molecule-level force:

$$\ln\frac{n_{2}}{n_{1}} = - \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T}.$$

The quantity

$$mg$$

appears as the energetic slope conjugate to the vertical distinction. Indeed,

$$- \frac{\partial\ln n}{\partial z} = \frac{mg}{k_{B}T}.$$

Thus the gravitational projection becomes directly readable as a thermodynamic slope. Part II develops the general thermodynamic-conjugate principle: intensive variables are energetic slopes with respect to readable extensive distinctions under specified boundary conditions.  In this jointly readable regime, gravity can therefore be represented as constraint per spatial distinction rather than beginning from primitive gravitational force.

Farther toward the gravitational horizon, where the thermodynamic comparison is lost, the field and geometric descriptions become indispensable again. Projection and deprojection therefore describe the same physical relation from opposite sides of a readability boundary.

### 11.15 Curvature Is Not Refuted by Deprojection
Nothing in this argument says that spacetime curvature is false. A projected geometric relation can be the most accurate and efficient physical representation available at a gravitational boundary. The claim is about causal priority.

If gravitational organization can be read directly as a thermodynamic occupancy relation when the relevant background remains accessible, then curvature is not required to be the primitive cause of that organization at every recursive boundary. Thus, curvature can be physically exact as a gravitational projection without implying curvature is primitive recursive ontology.

The atmosphere is useful precisely because it allows us to observe gravitational organization before the geometric projection becomes one-sided.

That is the deprojection test.

### 11.16 Bianconi Provides an Independent Mathematical Signpost
The present argument does not derive Einstein\'s field equation from R²D. That stronger problem remains open. There is, however, independent mathematical evidence that an entropic/statistical structure can induce a gravitational geometry.

Bianconi constructs a matter-induced topological metric

$$\widetilde{G} = \widetilde{g} + \alpha\widetilde{M} - \beta\widetilde{R},$$

where $\widetilde{M}$ is the matter-field contribution and $\widetilde{R}$ the curvature contribution. 

She then defines a statistical-mechanics action from the quantum relative entropy between the spacetime metric and the matter-induced metric. In dimensionless relative form, the action contains

$$- {{Tr}\ln}\left( \widetilde{G}{\widetilde{g}}^{-1} \right).$$

In the low-coupling limit, this entropic construction reduces to an Einstein--Hilbert action with matter, while the full construction produces modified Einstein equations. 

R²D does not presently identify Bianconi\'s induced metric with an R²D count operator. Nor does it identify her matter and curvature terms respectively with $P$ and $R$. The legitimate conclusion is narrower: a mathematically explicit statistical/entropic structure can generate an Einstein-like gravitational geometry.

That result is consistent with the direction proposed here:

$$\text{statistical/count~structure} \rightarrow \text{gravitational~geometric~projection}\text{.}$$

A rigorous operator-level bridge between R²D and such constructions is a problem for later work. It is not required for the present deprojection argument.

### 11.17 Gravity and Thermodynamics Are Not Separate Primitive Laws
The atmosphere now allows the relation between Parts II, III, and IV to be stated more sharply. Part II establishes thermodynamic mappings of the R²D count architecture. Part III shows that gravity emerges toward the enclosing side of the same recursive readability hierarchy. Part IV shows why a separate gravitational ontology becomes natural after the organizing recurrence relation is no longer jointly readable. The atmosphere completes the loop. At that boundary,

$$g$$

remains physically measurable. But so do

$$T, n, \Delta z, \text{molecular~recurrence}.$$

The gravitational relation can therefore be written directly in thermodynamic count form:

$$R_{atm} = \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T},$$

with occupancy

$$\frac{n_{2}}{n_{1}} = e^{- R_{atm}}.$$

Thus, gravity and thermodynamics need not be interpreted as two primitive physical laws accidentally meeting in the barometric equation. They can be different readability regimes of one recursive organization

$$\text{thermodynamics} = \text{joint~readability}$$

while

$$\text{gravity} = \text{one-sided~enclosing~projection}\text{.}$$

The difference is not the primitive mathematics. The difference is what remains readable.

### 11.18 Principle — Gravitational Deprojection
**Principle --- Gravitational Deprojection**

A gravitational relation becomes projected as a separate field or geometric ontology when retained large-scale distinguishability remains readable after the relevant enclosing recurrence relation can no longer be jointly resolved.

When the gravitationally retained distinction and the recurrence/background relation organizing its realization again become jointly readable, the same gravitational organization can acquire a direct thermodynamic count read.

For a dilute isothermal atmosphere,

$$R_{atm} = \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T},$$

and

$$\Delta\ln n = - R_{atm}.$$

If gravitational calibration is defined by background recurrence according to

$$G_{n} = \gamma_{G}\frac{c^{5}}{h\left( f_{G,n}^{\Phi} \right)^{2}},$$

then, for otherwise fixed atmospheric conditions,

$$R_{atm,n} \propto G_{n} \propto \left( f_{G,n}^{\Phi} \right)^{- 2}.$$

Thus, gravitational retention weakens relative to the thermal count scale toward recursively faster gravitational backgrounds.

Gravity has not been converted into heat and the gravitational relation has not disappeared. Its organization has become jointly readable in the thermodynamic count architecture.

### 11.19 Corollary — The Gravitational Constant Is a Readability Calibration
If

$$\gamma_{G}$$

is invariant while

$$f_{G}^{\Phi}$$

changes across recursively related gravitational backgrounds, then

$$G_{n}$$

is not the invariant physical object. It is the dimensional calibration appropriate to the background from which the gravitational relation is read. Thus, constant $G_{n}$ within a domain can indicate common gravitational background calibration. And

$$G_{n} \neq G_{n - 1}$$

need not indicate violation of a primitive universal law. It can indicate a change in the recursive background under which that law is physically represented.

The atmosphere supplies the complementary local consequence:

$$G_{n} \rightarrow R_{atm} \rightarrow \text{occupancy}\text{.}$$

The dimensional constant therefore acquires operational meaning through the count relation it calibrates.

### 11.20 Corollary — Deprojection Is Experimentally Constrained
The deprojection proposal is not protected from experiment by its abstract formulation. It makes concrete demands. The proposed gravitational scaling must be compatible with measured gravitational behavior within domains where $G$ is experimentally constant. Any transition to a different $G_{n}$ must correspond to a physically defensible change in recursive gravitational background rather than an arbitrary geometric scale. And wherever gravitational and thermal relations are jointly readable, the dimensionless retention

$$\frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T}$$

must continue to reproduce observed occupancy structure.

The theory therefore cannot choose recursive boundaries after observing discrepancies merely to preserve itself. The physical mapping must identify those boundaries independently. Likewise, the proposed relation

$$G_{n} \propto \left( f_{G,n}^{\Phi} \right)^{- 2}$$

must ultimately be tested against gravitational systems spanning multiple candidate recursive regimes.

The atmosphere does not prove that scaling law. It provides a particularly clean system in which the consequences of such a scaling can be stated.

### 11.21 What Has Been Established
Part IV began with hidden counting and visible physics. It showed that boundary-relative objects and fields can be physically real without being primitive. Mass and charge were placed as distinct coupling reads of boundary organization. Background supplied scale and frame before motion was defined. Fields emerged as physical reads of shared enclosing compatibility. Area preceded radius in the gravitational spatial projection. Radiation emerged as recurrent change in an enclosing relation. Quantum theory followed when recurrence remained after subordinate geometry became unreadable. Gauge invariance was interpreted as the physical projection of invariant counting structure. The argument was then extended beyond physics and finally to the largest cosmological projection.

The gravitational development added one further possibility. If gravitational background recurrence changes across recursive boundaries, dimensional gravitational calibration need not remain invariant:

$$G_{n} = \gamma_{G}\frac{c^{5}}{h\left( f_{G,n}^{\Phi} \right)^{2}}.$$

A slower enclosing background then produces a larger gravitational calibration. If a subordinate calibration is incorrectly extrapolated into that larger domain, the discrepancy may be projected as additional gravitational source or changed geometry.

But the same relation has another direction. Toward faster recursive backgrounds,

$$f_{G}^{\Phi} \uparrow \Rightarrow G \downarrow .$$

The atmosphere shows what happens as gravitational organization returns toward thermodynamic joint readability. There,

$$g \rightarrow mg\Delta z \rightarrow \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T} \rightarrow R_{atm} \rightarrow \text{occupancy}.$$

The separate gravitational projection does not become false. It becomes unnecessary as a primitive explanation because the relation that it had replaced becomes directly readable. Thus, the gravitational branch closes back onto the thermodynamic architecture:

$${\text{thermodynamic~joint~readability}
}{\downarrow
}{\text{l}\text{oss~of~enclosing~recurrence~readability}
}{\downarrow
}{\text{gravitational~field~and~geometry}
}{\downarrow_{\text{toward~faster~recursive~background}}
}{\text{joint~readability~restored}
}{\downarrow
}\text{thermodynamic~retention~and~occupancy}$$

The primitive structure has not changed. Only the physical read has.

### Final Principle — The Projected World Is Reversible in Readability
The central claim of Part IV can now be completed. A projected physical ontology arises when a recursively realized relation remains physically consequential after part of the count relation organizing it has become unreadable. A projected object, field, or geometry may therefore be experimentally exact within its boundary without being primitive across all boundaries. The converse also follows.

When the missing recursive relation again becomes jointly readable, the projected ontology can be deprojected into the more complete count relation from which it arose. For gravity, the atmosphere supplies the canonical example:

$$\text{gravitational~field~read} \rightarrow \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T} \rightarrow \text{thermodynamic~occupancy}\text{.}$$

Gravity has not vanished. Spacetime has not been disproved. Thermodynamics has not been promoted into a universal material substance. What has changed is what the boundary permits the observer to count.

The recursive hierarchy therefore does not consist of fundamentally different worlds stitched together by empirical laws. It consists of different readable projections of one boundary-indexed count architecture. Toward one horizon, distinction disappears and recurrence remains. Toward the other, recurrence disappears and distinction remains. Between them, both can be read.

That is why thermodynamics occupies the center of the theory. It is not merely another domain of physics. It is the place where the machine becomes visible. Projection begins when one side of the count goes dark. Deprojection begins when that side becomes readable again.

The atmosphere shows the gravitational return. The missing side of the count has come back.

## Conclusion --- Unification Before Units

The R²D program began from a deliberately simple proposition: counting requires distinguishability.

Before a force can be measured, before a field can be assigned, before an energy can be defined, before a particle can be counted, and before a spacetime coordinate can be constructed, there must already be a boundary within which alternatives are distinguishable.

That primitive structure is dimensionless. A boundary defines the count domain. Multiplicity defines the possible realizations of its states. Occurrence counts realized instances within that structure. Occupancy records how those realizations are distributed. Enclosing compatibility supplies directional possibility. Constraint determines retention. Their difference defines local asymmetry:

$$A = P - R.$$

Closure permits the same recursively organized realization to acquire a different count meaning at an enclosing boundary. The primitive architecture is therefore not mechanics, thermodynamics, quantum theory, or gravity. It is recursive counting.

The central question of the project has been whether the familiar domains of physics can be recovered as different physical readings of this one dimensionless architecture. The answer developed across Parts II--IV is increasingly clear.

**Thermodynamics Exposes the Count Geometry Directly**

Thermodynamics occupies a privileged position in R2D because it is the physical regime in which enough of the underlying recursive relation remains jointly readable.

Multiplicity remains visible through entropy:

$$S = \ln W.$$

Enclosing directional support becomes thermodynamically readable through Gibbs and chemical-potential relations. Retention acquires energetic reads through work-like constraint. Background becomes physically readable through thermal scale. Occupancy remains measurable. Recurrence remains measurable. The resulting thermodynamic equations therefore retain much of the primitive count grammar explicitly.

The R2D relation

$$A = P - R$$

does not first appear as a mechanical law.

It appears naturally as the dimensionless balance between available enclosing direction and the portion retained against distinguishable realization.

Temperature subsequently supplies a dimensional energetic calibration. Thus, thermodynamics does not generate the recursive count geometry. It exposes it.

$$\text{dimensionless~count~geometry} \rightarrow \text{thermodynamic~physical~read}\text{.}$$

This is why thermodynamics occupies the center of the R²D architecture. It is the regime of **joint readability**.

**Quantum Theory Is the Recurrence-Dominant Projection**

Move recursively toward subordinate closure. Recurrence becomes faster. Eventually the recurrence associated with a physical realization can remain readable after its subordinate distinguishability is no longer jointly accessible. The physical description changes. Continuous subordinate geometry recedes. What remains especially readable is:

$$\text{occurrence}, \text{occupancy}, \text{recurrence}, \text{relative~phase}.$$

The statistical forms encountered in quantum physics then emerge from count architecture rather than requiring particle ontology as their mathematical starting point. For repeatable occupancy,

$$\overline{n} = \frac{1}{e^{x} - 1}$$

has the Bose--Einstein form. For binary occupancy,

$$\overline{n} = \frac{1}{e^{x} + 1}$$

has the Fermi--Dirac form. The physical calibration subsequently introduces

$$E = hf$$

and the projected language of photons, particles, states, phase, and operators.

R²D has not thereby derived every structure of quantum mechanics. Spin-statistics theorem, the Standard Model spectrum, and other specifically quantum constructions remain further mathematical burdens. But the deepest statistical distinction conventionally used to separate bosonic and fermionic occupancy already belongs to dimensionless count structure. Thus, quantum theory approaches the same primitive architecture from one side:

$$\text{dimensionless~count~geometry} \rightarrow \text{recurrence-dominant~readability} \rightarrow \text{quantum~projection}\text{.}$$

**Gravity Is the Opposite Projection**

Move in the other recursive direction. Toward increasingly enclosing boundaries, recurrence is compressed. The physical distinction can remain readable after the recurrence of the larger organizing relation has become inaccessible. What survives is increasingly represented through

$$\text{mass}, \text{force}, \text{field}, \text{area}, \text{metric}, \text{curvature}.$$

Gravity therefore occupies the opposite readability limit from quantum theory.

Quantum physics retains recurrence after geometry becomes difficult to read. Gravity retains large-scale distinguishability after the relevant enclosing recurrence becomes difficult to read. The two are not therefore unrelated primitive ontologies. They are opposite one-sided reads of the same recursive architecture:

$$\begin{matrix}
\text{Quantum} & \text{Thermodynamics} & \text{Gravity} \\
\text{recurrence~dominant} & \text{joint~readability} & \text{distinguishability~dominant} \\
\text{subordinate~geometry~lost} & \text{both~readable} & \text{enclosing~recurrence~lost}.
\end{matrix}$$

This is the two-horizon structure of R²D.

**Gravity Can Be Followed Back Into the Count Geometry**

The gravitational branch remained incomplete until the same physical organization could be followed back toward thermodynamic joint readability. The atmosphere provides that return. Conventionally,

$$\frac{n(z + \Delta z)}{n(z)} = \exp\left( - \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T} \right).$$

The gravitationally organized spatial distinction therefore has the dimensionless thermodynamic read

$$R_{atm} = \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T}.$$

The occupancy relation becomes

$$\Delta\ln n = - R_{atm}$$

under the restricted equal-native-multiplicity, equal-possibility comparison.

Here the gravitational field has not disappeared. It has become jointly readable with the thermal recurrence/background relation against which its occupancy consequence is measured.

The projected gravitational ontology therefore deprojects into the primitive thermodynamic grammar:

$$g \rightarrow mg\Delta z \rightarrow \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T} \rightarrow R \rightarrow \text{occupancy}\text{.}$$

This is the missing return path.

Gravity is not merely philosophically compatible with the count architecture. At the thermodynamic boundary, its observable organization is directly expressible within it.

**A Boundary-Dependent **$\mathbf{G}$** Strengthens the Connection**

The gravitational projection also suggests that the dimensional gravitational constant need not itself be the invariant object. If the gravitational background recurrence at boundary $B_{n}$ is

$$f_{G,n}^{\Phi},$$

and the invariant dimensionless gravitational calibration is

$$\gamma_{G},$$

then the proposed physical scaling is

$$G_{n} = \gamma_{G}\frac{c^{5}}{h\left( f_{G,n}^{\Phi} \right)^{2}}.$$

Hence, between recursively related gravitational backgrounds,

$$\frac{G_{n}}{G_{n - 1}} = \left( \frac{f_{G,n - 1}^{\Phi}}{f_{G,n}^{\Phi}} \right)^{2}.$$

Slower enclosing recurrence corresponds to stronger dimensional gravitational calibration. Faster subordinate recurrence corresponds to weaker dimensional gravitational calibration. The invariant relation can therefore remain unchanged while the dimensional physical constant changes. This provides a concrete example of a broader R²D principle: what remains invariant across recursive boundaries need not be a dimensional physical quantity.

The more primitive invariant may be dimensionless. That observation matters directly to unification.

**Physical Laws Need Not Be Unified in Their Projected Coordinates**

The conventional ambition of grand unification is often phrased as the search for a common physical theory of apparently different interactions. That ambition is understandable if

$$\text{particle}, \text{mass}, \text{charge}, \text{energy}, \text{field}, \text{force}, \text{spacetime}$$

are assumed to be primitive objects. R²D changes the logical problem.

These quantities arise only after a recursive relation has been physically read from a specified boundary. Different boundaries preserve different aspects of the underlying architecture. They may therefore produce physical descriptions having different units, different objects, different mathematical coordinates, and even apparently different causal ontologies. There is no reason to expect those projected quantities themselves to become identical. Mass need not become charge. Gravity need not become electromagnetism. A particle need not become curvature. Temperature need not become time.

Trying to unify those physical coordinates directly may therefore be asking the wrong question. The appropriate question is: What dimensionless mathematical structure exists before these different physical readings are assigned? Part I supplies the primitive structural answer within R²D: boundary-indexed recursive count geometry. The remaining burden is to determine whether the proposed domain-specific physical mappings correctly read that architecture.

**The Unification Is Beneath Quantum Theory, Thermodynamics, and Gravity**

The emerging architecture can be written compactly: dimensionless recursive count geometry branches physically into thermodynamic joint readability, quantum recurrence-dominated projection, and gravitational distinguishability-dominant projection.

The evidence for the three branches is different. Thermodynamics supplies the most direct dimensional mapping of the count architecture. Quantum theory exposes the same architecture through occupancy statistics and recurrence. Gravity can be followed backward through the atmosphere into a dimensionless thermodynamic retention relation.

The gravitational derivation remains less complete than the thermodynamic mapping. R²D has not derived the Einstein field equation from its primitive axioms, nor should the present manuscript claim that it has. But that stronger result is no longer required to establish the central architectural point.

The gravitational projection has a thermodynamic limit in which its organizing relation becomes directly readable as dimensionless count bias. Thus, the triangle closes:

$$\begin{matrix}
& \text{Dimensionless~Recursive~Count~Geometry} & \\
\swarrow & & \searrow \\
\text{Quantum} & & \text{Gravity} \\
\searrow & & \swarrow \\
& \text{Thermodynamics} &
\end{matrix}$$

Thermodynamics is not one more corner of the triangle. It is the region where the structure underlying the other two remains jointly visible.

**Grand Unification Is Therefore a Pre-Dimensional Problem**

The central conclusion follows. If quantum theory, thermodynamics, and gravity are physical readings of different readability regimes of the same recursive architecture, then their common structure cannot first be sought among their dimensional physical coordinates.

Those coordinates have already been assigned **after** the primitive relation has been projected. Therefore, within R²D, grand unification must occur before dimensionalization. Or more simply, grand unification occurs before units.

This is not a statement that dimensional physics is mistaken. Units are indispensable once a physical relation has been assigned a readable coordinate. The claim concerns logical order. The primitive mathematics comes first. Physical units come later.

The same dimensionless recursive relation can therefore acquire one physical read as thermodynamic energy, another as quantum recurrence, another as gravitational geometry, and still another as mechanical work.

What is unified is not the physical vocabulary. It is the count grammar from which that vocabulary emerges.

**Principle --- Unification Before Units**

A physical quantity acquires units only after a dimensionless recursively realized count relation has received a boundary-specific physical read. Different readability regimes may therefore represent the same primitive count architecture through different physical quantities, units, and projected ontologies. Thermodynamics exposes the architecture where distinguishability and recurrence remain jointly readable.

Quantum theory represents the recurrence-dominant side after subordinate distinguishability becomes inaccessible. Gravity represents the distinguishability-dominant side after the relevant enclosing recurrence becomes inaccessible. When gravitational distinguishability and thermal recurrence again become jointly readable, as in the atmospheric relation

$$R_{atm} = \frac{mg\Delta z}{k_{B}T}$$

the gravitational projection deprojects into the same dimensionless count architecture. Therefore the common structure beneath quantum theory, thermodynamics, and gravity is not a common particle, force, field, energy, or spacetime geometry. It is dimensionless recursive count geometry.

Consequently, if R²D is correct, the path to grand unification lies beneath units.

**The Path Is Now Clear**

The remaining program is no longer conceptually ambiguous. It is quantitative. R²D must continue to test whether the same primitive count relations can reproduce the physical mappings already identified without changing their underlying definitions from one domain to another.

The thermodynamic mappings must remain exact. The quantum occupancy mappings must reproduce the full statistics and eventually connect rigorously to phase, measurement, and quantum state structure. The gravitational scaling relation must survive empirical comparison. The relation between recursive count geometry and Einsteinian geometry remains to be derived if the stronger gravitational program is to be completed.

These are substantial mathematical and experimental burdens. But they are no longer separate searches for three unrelated foundations. They are tests of one physical mapping program: how do the different domain-specific laws arise as reads of the already-established primitive count architecture, and which proposed mappings survive quantitative test?

If the answer is yes, grand unification does not require forcing quantum mechanics and gravity into one already-dimensional ontology. Their apparent incompatibility is partly the expected consequence of comparing opposite projections. The common theory lies underneath both.

Thermodynamics shows it while both sides remain visible. Quantum theory shows what remains when one side disappears. Gravity shows what remains when the other does. And the atmosphere shows that the gravitational projection can be followed back.

The path therefore closes where the project began:

$$\text{boundary} \rightarrow \text{distinction} \rightarrow \text{change} \rightarrow \text{recurrence} \rightarrow \text{count}\text{.}$$

Everything after that is a physical read. The unification is in the counting.
